@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..0be6dc6d49ef4f738c78fa80f54297709dbd9220
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_tests_use_isolated_database(app, database_path, tmp_path):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert database_path != str(tmp_path / "instance" / "campus_shop.sqlite")

diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..169c94a1b76d0e263c3ceaaed47f8ae4c3d865fa
--- /dev/null
+++ b/README.md
@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..0be6dc6d49ef4f738c78fa80f54297709dbd9220
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_tests_use_isolated_database(app, database_path, tmp_path):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert database_path != str(tmp_path / "instance" / "campus_shop.sqlite")

@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
+    name TEXT NOT NULL,
+    description TEXT NOT NULL,
+    category TEXT NOT NULL,
+    price_cents INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents > 0),
+    image TEXT
+);
+
+INSERT OR IGNORE INTO product
+    (id, name, description, category, price_cents, image)
+VALUES
+    (1, 'Campus Notebook', 'A ruled notebook for lectures and study sessions.', 'Study Supplies', 499, NULL),
+    (2, 'Black Ink Pen Set', 'A set of three smooth-writing black ink pens.', 'Study Supplies', 349, NULL),
+    (3, 'Pastel Highlighters', 'Four pastel highlighters for notes and textbooks.', 'Study Supplies', 599, NULL),
+    (4, 'Reusable Water Bottle', 'A lightweight bottle for days around campus.', 'Campus Essentials', 1299, NULL),
+    (5, 'Canvas Book Tote', 'A sturdy canvas tote for books and daily supplies.', 'Campus Essentials', 1499, NULL),
+    (6, 'USB-C Charging Cable', 'A one-metre cable for compatible phones and laptops.', 'Tech Accessories', 899, NULL);

+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def db(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        connection = get_db()
+        yield connection
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def database_path(app):
+    return app.config["DATABASE"]
diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..0be6dc6d49ef4f738c78fa80f54297709dbd9220
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_tests_use_isolated_database(app, database_path, tmp_path):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert database_path != str(tmp_path / "instance" / "campus_shop.sqlite")

@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
+    name TEXT NOT NULL,
+    description TEXT NOT NULL,
+    category TEXT NOT NULL,
+    price_cents INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents > 0),
+    image TEXT
+);
+
+INSERT OR IGNORE INTO product
+    (id, name, description, category, price_cents, image)
+VALUES
+    (1, 'Campus Notebook', 'A ruled notebook for lectures and study sessions.', 'Study Supplies', 499, NULL),
+    (2, 'Black Ink Pen Set', 'A set of three smooth-writing black ink pens.', 'Study Supplies', 349, NULL),
+    (3, 'Pastel Highlighters', 'Four pastel highlighters for notes and textbooks.', 'Study Supplies', 599, NULL),
+    (4, 'Reusable Water Bottle', 'A lightweight bottle for days around campus.', 'Campus Essentials', 1299, NULL),
+    (5, 'Canvas Book Tote', 'A sturdy canvas tote for books and daily supplies.', 'Campus Essentials', 1499, NULL),
+    (6, 'USB-C Charging Cable', 'A one-metre cable for compatible phones and laptops.', 'Tech Accessories', 899, NULL);

+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_tests_use_isolated_database(app, database_path, tmp_path):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert database_path != str(tmp_path / "instance" / "campus_shop.sqlite")

new file mode 100644

+++ b/README.md

+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
+    name TEXT NOT NULL,
+    description TEXT NOT NULL,
+    category TEXT NOT NULL,
+    price_cents INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents > 0),
+    image TEXT
+);
+
+INSERT OR IGNORE INTO product
+    (id, name, description, category, price_cents, image)
+VALUES
+    (1, 'Campus Notebook', 'A ruled notebook for lectures and study sessions.', 'Study Supplies', 499, NULL),
+    (2, 'Black Ink Pen Set', 'A set of three smooth-writing black ink pens.', 'Study Supplies', 349, NULL),
+    (3, 'Pastel Highlighters', 'Four pastel highlighters for notes and textbooks.', 'Study Supplies', 599, NULL),
+    (4, 'Reusable Water Bottle', 'A lightweight bottle for days around campus.', 'Campus Essentials', 1299, NULL),
+    (5, 'Canvas Book Tote', 'A sturdy canvas tote for books and daily supplies.', 'Campus Essentials', 1499, NULL),
+    (6, 'USB-C Charging Cable', 'A one-metre cable for compatible phones and laptops.', 'Tech Accessories', 899, NULL);

diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..0be6dc6d49ef4f738c78fa80f54297709dbd9220
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_tests_use_isolated_database(app, database_path, tmp_path):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert database_path != str(tmp_path / "instance" / "campus_shop.sqlite")

new file mode 100644

--- /dev/null
+++ b/README.md
@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
+    name TEXT NOT NULL,
+    description TEXT NOT NULL,
+    category TEXT NOT NULL,
+    price_cents INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents > 0),
+    image TEXT
+);
+
+INSERT OR IGNORE INTO product
+    (id, name, description, category, price_cents, image)
+VALUES
+    (1, 'Campus Notebook', 'A ruled notebook for lectures and study sessions.', 'Study Supplies', 499, NULL),
+    (2, 'Black Ink Pen Set', 'A set of three smooth-writing black ink pens.', 'Study Supplies', 349, NULL),
+    (3, 'Pastel Highlighters', 'Four pastel highlighters for notes and textbooks.', 'Study Supplies', 599, NULL),
+    (4, 'Reusable Water Bottle', 'A lightweight bottle for days around campus.', 'Campus Essentials', 1299, NULL),
+    (5, 'Canvas Book Tote', 'A sturdy canvas tote for books and daily supplies.', 'Campus Essentials', 1499, NULL),
+    (6, 'USB-C Charging Cable', 'A one-metre cable for compatible phones and laptops.', 'Tech Accessories', 899, NULL);

+import sqlite3
+
+import pytest

+from campus_shop import create_app
+from campus_shop.db import get_db, init_db

+def app(tmp_path):

+    app = create_app(
+        {
+            "TESTING": True,
+            "DATABASE": str(database_path),
+            "SECRET_KEY": "test-secret",
+        }
+    )
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        init_db()
+
+    yield app
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def client(app):
+    return app.test_client()
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def db(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        connection = get_db()
+        yield connection
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def database_path(app):
+    return app.config["DATABASE"]
diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..0be6dc6d49ef4f738c78fa80f54297709dbd9220
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_tests_use_isolated_database(app, database_path, tmp_path):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert database_path != str(tmp_path / "instance" / "campus_shop.sqlite")

+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_tests_use_isolated_database(app, database_path, tmp_path):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert database_path != str(tmp_path / "instance" / "campus_shop.sqlite")

+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
+    name TEXT NOT NULL,

+    category TEXT NOT NULL,
+    price_cents INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents > 0),
+    image TEXT
+);
+
+INSERT OR IGNORE INTO product
+    (id, name, description, category, price_cents, image)
+VALUES
+    (1, 'Campus Notebook', 'A ruled notebook for lectures and study sessions.', 'Study Supplies', 499, NULL),
+    (2, 'Black Ink Pen Set', 'A set of three smooth-writing black ink pens.', 'Study Supplies', 349, NULL),
+    (3, 'Pastel Highlighters', 'Four pastel highlighters for notes and textbooks.', 'Study Supplies', 599, NULL),
+    (4, 'Reusable Water Bottle', 'A lightweight bottle for days around campus.', 'Campus Essentials', 1299, NULL),
+    (5, 'Canvas Book Tote', 'A sturdy canvas tote for books and daily supplies.', 'Campus Essentials', 1499, NULL),
+    (6, 'USB-C Charging Cable', 'A one-metre cable for compatible phones and laptops.', 'Tech Accessories', 899, NULL);

diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..0be6dc6d49ef4f738c78fa80f54297709dbd9220
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,81 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_tests_use_isolated_database(app, database_path, tmp_path):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert database_path != str(tmp_path / "instance" / "campus_shop.sqlite")

+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and

+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f

+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
+    name TEXT NOT NULL,
+    description TEXT NOT NULL,
+    category TEXT NOT NULL,
+    price_cents INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents > 0),
+    image TEXT
+);
+
+INSERT OR IGNORE INTO product
+    (id, name, description, category, price_cents, image)
+VALUES
+    (1, 'Campus Notebook', 'A ruled notebook for lectures and study sessions.', 'Study Supplies', 499, NULL),
+    (2, 'Black Ink Pen Set', 'A set of three smooth-writing black ink pens.', 'Study Supplies', 349, NULL),
+    (3, 'Pastel Highlighters', 'Four pastel highlighters for notes and textbooks.', 'Study Supplies', 599, NULL),
+    (4, 'Reusable Water Bottle', 'A lightweight bottle for days around campus.', 'Campus Essentials', 1299, NULL),
+    (5, 'Canvas Book Tote', 'A sturdy canvas tote for books and daily supplies.', 'Campus Essentials', 1499, NULL),
+    (6, 'USB-C Charging Cable', 'A one-metre cable for compatible phones and laptops.', 'Tech Accessories', 899, NULL);

diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..46ac342214772ba81339fc921d570bdaab9191fb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,85 @@
+import sqlite3
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_application_factory_uses_isolated_test_configuration(
+    app, database_path, tmp_path
+):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert Path(database_path).parent == tmp_path
+    assert Path(app.instance_path).is_dir()

"C:\\Windows\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command "& '.\\.venv\\Scripts\\python.exe' -m compileall -q campus_shop tests; @'
import sqlite3
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

schema = Path('campus_shop/schema.sql').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
    database = Path(directory) / 'verify.sqlite'
    connection = sqlite3.connect(database)
    connection.executescript(schema)
    first = connection.execute('SELECT id, name, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id').fetchall()
    connection.executescript(schema)
    second = connection.execute('SELECT id, name, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id').fetchall()
    columns = connection.execute('PRAGMA table_info(product)').fetchall()
    assert len(first) == 6
    assert len({row[2] for row in first}) >= 2
    assert all(row[3] > 0 for row in first)
    assert second == first
    assert [row[1] for row in columns] == ['id', 'name', 'description', 'category', 'price_cents', 'image']
    connection.close()
print('schema verification passed: 6 products, 3 categories, idempotent reseed')

+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..46ac342214772ba81339fc921d570bdaab9191fb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,85 @@
+import sqlite3
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_application_factory_uses_isolated_test_configuration(
+    app, database_path, tmp_path
+):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert Path(database_path).parent == tmp_path
+    assert Path(app.instance_path).is_dir()

+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app

diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..46ac342214772ba81339fc921d570bdaab9191fb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,85 @@
+import sqlite3
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_application_factory_uses_isolated_test_configuration(
+    app, database_path, tmp_path
+):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert Path(database_path).parent == tmp_path
+    assert Path(app.instance_path).is_dir()

@@ -0,0 +1,30 @@
+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
+    name TEXT NOT NULL,
+    description TEXT NOT NULL,
+    category TEXT NOT NULL,
+    price_cents INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents > 0),
+    image TEXT
+);
+
+INSERT OR IGNORE INTO product
+    (id, name, description, category, price_cents, image)
+VALUES
+    (1, 'Campus Notebook', 'A ruled notebook for lectures and study sessions.', 'Study Supplies', 499, NULL),
+    (2, 'Black Ink Pen Set', 'A set of three smooth-writing black ink pens.', 'Study Supplies', 349, NULL),
+    (3, 'Pastel Highlighters', 'Four pastel highlighters for notes and textbooks.', 'Study Supplies', 599, NULL),
+    (4, 'Reusable Water Bottle', 'A lightweight bottle for days around campus.', 'Campus Essentials', 1299, NULL),
+    (5, 'Canvas Book Tote', 'A sturdy canvas tote for books and daily supplies.', 'Campus Essentials', 1499, NULL),

+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.
diff --git a/campus_shop/__init__.py b/campus_shop/__init__.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..44f0798d47d061d09651d0fdaa3343b393ba0d9f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/__init__.py
@@ -0,0 +1,25 @@
+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4d225895a2bc577a7686d518ed2c4b9984bad7e6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/campus_shop/db.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+import sqlite3
+
+import click
+from flask import current_app, g
+
+
+def get_db():
+    """Return the request-local SQLite connection."""
+    if "db" not in g:
+        g.db = sqlite3.connect(
+            current_app.config["DATABASE"],
+            detect_types=sqlite3.PARSE_DECLTYPES,
+        )
+        g.db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
+
+    return g.db
+
+
+def close_db(error=None):
+    """Close the request-local connection, if one was opened."""
+    db = g.pop("db", None)
+
+    if db is not None:
+        db.close()
+
+
+def init_db():
+    """Create missing tables and insert any missing stable seed rows."""
+    db = get_db()
+
+    with current_app.open_resource("schema.sql") as schema_file:
+        db.executescript(schema_file.read().decode("utf-8"))
+
+
+@click.command("init-db")
+def init_db_command():
+    """Create the database schema and seed the catalog."""
+    init_db()
+    click.echo("Initialized the database.")
+
+
+def init_app(app):
+    app.teardown_appcontext(close_db)
+    app.cli.add_command(init_db_command)

+CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product (
+    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
+    name TEXT NOT NULL,
+    description TEXT NOT NULL,
+    category TEXT NOT NULL,
+    price_cents INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents > 0),
+    image TEXT
+);
+
+INSERT OR IGNORE INTO product
+    (id, name, description, category, price_cents, image)
+VALUES
+    (1, 'Campus Notebook', 'A ruled notebook for lectures and study sessions.', 'Study Supplies', 499, NULL),
+    (2, 'Black Ink Pen Set', 'A set of three smooth-writing black ink pens.', 'Study Supplies', 349, NULL),
+    (3, 'Pastel Highlighters', 'Four pastel highlighters for notes and textbooks.', 'Study Supplies', 599, NULL),
+    (4, 'Reusable Water Bottle', 'A lightweight bottle for days around campus.', 'Campus Essentials', 1299, NULL),
+    (5, 'Canvas Book Tote', 'A sturdy canvas tote for books and daily supplies.', 'Campus Essentials', 1499, NULL),
+    (6, 'USB-C Charging Cable', 'A one-metre cable for compatible phones and laptops.', 'Tech Accessories', 899, NULL);

diff --git a/tests/test_database.py b/tests/test_database.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..46ac342214772ba81339fc921d570bdaab9191fb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_database.py
@@ -0,0 +1,85 @@
+import sqlite3
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from campus_shop.db import get_db
+
+
+EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
+    "id": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 0, "pk": 1},
+    "name": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "description": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "category": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "price_cents": {"type": "INTEGER", "notnull": 1, "pk": 0},
+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}
+
+
+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+
+    assert [tuple(row) for row in after] == before_values
+
+
+def test_application_factory_uses_isolated_test_configuration(
+    app, database_path, tmp_path
+):
+    assert app.config["TESTING"] is True
+    assert app.config["SECRET_KEY"] == "test-secret"
+    assert Path(database_path).parent == tmp_path
+    assert Path(app.instance_path).is_dir()

Group 1 implementation and tests are present. Static compilation, schema/idempotency checks, and diff checks passed, but runtime pytest verification is blocked because Flask/pytest cannot be installed in the network-restricted sandbox. Only independently verified checklist items were checked.

+    "image": {"type": "TEXT", "notnull": 0, "pk": 0},
+}

+def test_product_schema_has_required_columns_and_constraints(db):
+    columns = {
+        row["name"]: {
+            "type": row["type"],
+            "notnull": row["notnull"],
+            "pk": row["pk"],
+        }
+        for row in db.execute("PRAGMA table_info(product)").fetchall()
+    }
+
+    assert columns == EXPECTED_COLUMNS
+
+    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
+        db.execute(
+            """
+            INSERT INTO product
+                (id, name, description, category, price_cents)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            """,
+            (99, "Invalid", "Invalid price test", "Test", 0),
+        )
+
+
+def test_seed_data_is_complete(db):
+    products = db.execute(
+        "SELECT id, name, description, category, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+    ).fetchall()
+
+    assert len(products) >= 6
+    assert len({product["id"] for product in products}) == len(products)
+    assert len({product["category"] for product in products}) >= 2
+    assert all(product["name"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["description"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["category"] for product in products)
+    assert all(product["price_cents"] > 0 for product in products)
+
+
+def test_init_db_command_is_idempotent(app):
+    with app.app_context():
+        before = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"
+        ).fetchall()
+        before_values = [tuple(row) for row in before]
+
+    runner = app.test_cli_runner()
+    first_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+    second_result = runner.invoke(args=["init-db"])
+
+    assert first_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert second_result.exit_code == 0
+    assert "Initialized the database." in second_result.output
+
+    with app.app_context():
+        after = get_db().execute(
+            "SELECT id, name, price_cents FROM product ORDER BY id"

+# Campus Mini Shop
+
+Campus Mini Shop is a small Flask application for browsing campus supplies and
+building a temporary session cart. The base system runs entirely on a local
+computer with SQLite; it does not include accounts, checkout, orders, or
+payment.
+
+## Local setup
+
+From the repository root in PowerShell:
+
+```powershell
+py -m venv .venv
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install -r requirements.txt
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop init-db
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m flask --app campus_shop run --debug
+```
+
+Open `http://127.0.0.1:5000/` after the server starts. The `init-db` command is
+safe to run again: it creates any missing schema and seed rows without
+duplicating or deleting existing products.
+
+## Tests
+
+```powershell
+.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Tests use a fresh temporary SQLite database and do not modify the local
+development database in `instance/`.

+import os
+
+from flask import Flask
+
+
+def create_app(test_config=None):
+    """Create and configure the Campus Mini Shop application."""
+    app = Flask(__name__, instance_relative_config=True)
+    app.config.from_mapping(
+        DATABASE=os.path.join(app.instance_path, "campus_shop.sqlite"),
+        SECRET_KEY=os.environ.get(
+            "CAMPUS_SHOP_SECRET_KEY", "dev-only-change-this-secret"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    if test_config is not None:
+        app.config.from_mapping(test_config)
+
+    os.makedirs(app.instance_path, exist_ok=True)
+
+    from . import db
+
+    db.init_app(app)
+
+    return app
diff --git a/campus_shop/db.py b/campus_shop/db.py
